function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

let readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;
  readonlyRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync || !window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)return false;
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
  try {
    if(force){
      const sync=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
      if(!sync?.ok) throw new Error(sync?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패');
    }
    const cached=await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if(!cached?.ok) throw new Error(cached?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.');
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError='';
    if(typeof studentBadgeCache!=='undefined'&&studentBadgeCache?.clear) studentBadgeCache.clear();
    updateDormOutingCount();
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if(rerender && !setupWizardActive){
      const scrollY=window.scrollY;
      render(state.activePage||'dashboard');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo(0,scrollY));
    }
    return true;
  } catch(error) {
    googleConnectionError=error?.message||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패';
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }
  })();
  try{return await readonlyRefreshInFlight;}finally{readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;}
}

async function restoreRememberedSessionImmediately(){
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberUser?state?.auth?.rememberedUser:null;
  const savedUser=state?.auth?.user;
  if(!uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered?.name)||!remembered?.email||!uep08125ValidTeacherName(savedUser?.name)||!savedUser?.email)return false;
  const sameName=String(savedUser.name).trim()===String(remembered.name).trim();
  const sameEmail=String(savedUser.email).trim().toLowerCase()===String(remembered.email).trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!sameName||!sameEmail)return false;
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus)return false;

===== FILE: app/resources/app/gyomuon.js | PATTERN: previewReadonlySync =====

  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

let readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;
  readonlyRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync || !window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)return false;
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
  try {
    if(force){
      const sync=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
      if(!sync?.ok) throw new Error(sync?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패');
    }
    const cached=await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if(!cached?.ok) throw new Error(cached?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.');
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError='';
    if(typeof studentBadgeCache!=='undefined'&&studentBadgeCache?.clear) studentBadgeCache.clear();
    updateDormOutingCount();
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if(rerender && !setupWizardActive){
      const scrollY=window.scrollY;
      render(state.activePage||'dashboard');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo(0,scrollY));
    }
    return true;
  } catch(error) {
    googleConnectionError=error?.message||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패';
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }
  })();
  try{return await readonlyRefreshInFlight;}finally{readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;}
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");

    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

let readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;
  readonlyRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync || !window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)return false;
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
  try {
    if(force){

function formatNeisYmd(text) {
  const value = String(text || "");
  return /^\d{8}$/.test(value) ? `${Number(value.slice(4, 6))}/${Number(value.slice(6, 8))}` : value;
}

function periodTimeLabel(period) {
  const row = (state.settings.schoolHours || []).filter((item) => String(item?.[0] || "").includes("교시"))[Number(period) - 1];
  return row ? `${row[1]}~${row[2]}` : "";
}

function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
  if(!/^\d{8}$/.test(text))return null;
  const date=new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)),Number(text.slice(4,6))-1,Number(text.slice(6,8)),12,0,0);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
}
function expectedSchoolPeriods(value){
  const date=value instanceof Date?value:neisDateToLocalDate(value);
  const weekdayNo=date?.getDay();
  return weekdayNo===3?5:([1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo)?7:0);
}

function uep08123TeacherKey(value){
  return String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/(선생님|교사)$/,'').trim();
}
function uep08123FirstArray(...values){
  for(const value of values){ if(Array.isArray(value)&&value.length) return value; }
  return [];
}
function uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(data){
  if(!data||typeof data!=='object') return data;
  if(!Array.isArray(data.timetableRows)||!data.timetableRows.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.timetableMaster,data.masterTimetableRows,data.teacherTimetableRows,data.timetable?.rows,data.timetable?.timetableRows,data.timetable?.masterRows);
    if(rows.length) data.timetableRows=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.notices)||!data.notices.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.workNotices,data.directNotices,data.schoolNotices,data.work?.notices,data.operations?.notices);
    if(rows.length) data.notices=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.dormOutings)||!data.dormOutings.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.dormOutingRows,data.outings,data.dorm?.outings,data.dormitory?.outings);
    if(rows.length) data.dormOutings=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.schoolDeadlines)||!data.schoolDeadlines.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.deadlines,data.workDeadlines,data.work?.deadlines);
    if(rows.length) data.schoolDeadlines=rows;
  }
  if(Array.isArray(data.officialAttendance))data.officialAttendance=data.officialAttendance.map((row,index)=>({
    ...row,
    id:String(row.id||row.attendanceId||row['출결ID']||`official-${index}`).trim(),
    studentId:String(row.studentId||row['학생ID']||'').trim(),
    studentNo:String(row.studentNo||row['학번']||'').replace(/\.0$/,''),
    name:String(row.name||row['성명']||row['학생명']||'').trim(),
    className:String(row.className||row.classNo||row['반']||'').replace(/\.0$/,''),
    date:uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day||row.rawDate||row['일자']||row['출결일자']||row['출결일']),
    type:String(row.type||[row['출결구분'],row['세부구분']].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'공결'),
    period:String(row.period||row['인정범위']||[row['시작교시'],row['종료교시']].filter(Boolean).join('~')||'하루 전체'),
    reason:String(row.reason||[row['사유'],row['장소']].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')),
    evidence:String(row.evidence||row['증빙상태']||''),
    sourceTab:String(row.sourceTab||row['원본탭']||'30_공결기록')
  }));
  return data;
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

      } else {
        googleConnectionError='';

function uepSelectionStableStudentKey(row={}){
  const sid=String(row.studentId??row.studentID??row['학생ID']??'').trim();
  if(sid) return `ID:${sid}`;
  const sno=String(row.studentNo??row.studentNumber??row['학번']??'').trim();
  return sno?`NO:${sno}`:'';
}

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}

function inputCenterExpandTextFile(file){
  const raw=String(file?.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
  const structured=inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file,raw);
  const classification=inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file,raw);
  if(structured){structured.forEach(d=>{const q=(d.sheetHeaders||[]).filter(h=>/^\s*\d+[.)]/.test(String(h||''))).length;const isReport=q>=3||/보고서|제출내역_텍스트/.test(String(d.sheetName||''));d.detectedType=isReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=isReport?'높음':classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=isReport?`Excel 문항형 보고서 · ${q}개 문항 직접 인식`:classification.reason;});return structured;}
  if(!raw.trim()){const d=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,'');d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;return [d];}
  const lines=raw.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
  const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
  const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
  drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;
}

function showConnectionGuide(name, message) {
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "CONNECTION";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = name;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<div class="connection-guide"><span>⚙</span><h3>연결 설정이 필요합니다</h3><p>${message}</p><small>주소나 실행 경로를 임의로 추정하지 않습니다. 설정값 확인 후 연결합니다.</small></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}

function openConnectionDetail(key) {
  const item = state.settings.dataConnections?.[key];
  if (!item) return;
  const columns = key === "studentMaster"
    ? "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 출신학교 · 입학자료 · 학생/보호자 연락처 · 생년월일 · 비고"
    : "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 학사 방 · 퇴사 · 입사 · 학기별 변동";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "READ ONLY SOURCE";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = item.label;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="connection-detail"><div><b>원본 파일</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.fileTitle)}</span></div><div><b>대상 탭</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.sheetName)}</span></div><div><b>머리글 행</b><span>${item.headerRow}행</span></div><div><b>결합 기준</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.joinKey)}</span></div><div><b>확인된 열</b><span>${columns}</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>현재 상태</b><span>구조 확인 완료 · OAuth 인증 전 · 실제 행 미저장</span></div><p class="drawer-note">동기화 시 학급+번호가 같더라도 성명이 다르면 자동 결합하지 않고 오류 목록으로 분리합니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}

function openSchoolCalendarEvent(id) {
  const event = allSchoolEvents().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!event) return;
  const date = new Date(`${event.date}T00:00:00`);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "SCHOOL CALENDAR";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = event.title;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="calendar-event-fields"><div><b>대상</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.target || "원문 확인")}</span></div><div><b>시간</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.time || "종일·미정")}</span></div><div><b>장소</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.place || "원문 확인")}</span></div><div><b>프로그램명</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.title)}</span></div><div><b>부서·담당자</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.owner || "원문 확인")}</span></div></div><div class="connection-detail"><div><b>날짜</b><span>${date.getFullYear()}년 ${date.getMonth() + 1}월 ${date.getDate()}일</span></div><div><b>구분</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.type)}</span></div><div><b>상태</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.status || "상태 미기재")}</span></div><div><b>출처</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.source)}</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>일정 원문</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.detail)}</span></div><div class="drawer-actions calendar-detail-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-calendar-edit="${escapeHtml(event.id)}">⚙ 일정 수정·보완</button></div><p class="drawer-note">수정 내용은 41_학교캘린더의 UEP 보정값으로 저장되며, 월간·주간·오늘의 프로그램에 공통 반영됩니다. 원본 업무일정 시트는 직접 변경하지 않습니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  const editButton=$("#drawerBody [data-calendar-edit]");
  if(editButton) editButton.onclick=()=>openCalendarEditDrawer(editButton.dataset.calendarEdit);
}

function normalizeProgramTitle(value) {
  return String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^0-9a-z가-힣]/g, "");
}

function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
  const kind=String(program.kind||"");
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
  if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
  // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
  if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
  if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
  if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
  // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
  if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
  // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
  if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
    const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
    if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function dashboardReportGroupState(group){
  const total=(group?.rows||[]).length;
  const submitted=(group?.rows||[]).filter(row=>row.submitted===true).length;
  return {total,submitted,missing:Math.max(0,total-submitted),state:total>0&&submitted===total?"complete":submitted>0?"progress":"missing"};
}
function dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const kind=String(program.kind||program.activityKind||program.programKind||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const label=[program.activityType,program.programType,program.category,program.group,program.type].map(value=>String(value||'')).join(' ');
  if(kind==='common'||kind==='after'||/공통활동|공통\s*프로그램|방과후/.test(label))return false;
  return kind==='selected'||kind==='general'||/선택활동|선택\s*프로그램/.test(label);
}
function dashboardReportStatusRows(){
  const rows=[];
  const programs=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(program=>dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(program));
  const reports=readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[];
  programs.forEach(program=>{
    const programTitle=String(program.recordTitle||program.actualTitle||program.title||'프로그램명 확인 필요').trim();
    (program.students||[]).forEach(student=>{
      // UEP 0.82.05: explicit report setting/form/data connection, or an actually submitted report,
      // is the only evidence that this student-program pair belongs to the report target population.
      if(!programRequiresStudentReport(program,student,reports))return;
      const matchedReport=resolveStudentProgramReport(program,student,reports);
      const submitted=Boolean(matchedReport&&matchedReport.submitted!==false);
      rows.push({
        studentId:student.studentId||student.id||'',
        studentNo:student.studentNo||student.number||'',
        name:student.name||student.studentName||'',
        classNo:student.classNo||student.className||'',
        programId:program.programId||program.id||'',
        programTitle,
        activityKind:'선택활동',
        submitted,
        reportId:matchedReport?.id||'',
        reportRef:matchedReport||null,
        status:submitted?'제출 완료':'미제출'
      });
    });
  });
  if(rows.length)return rows;
  // Fallback: if program roster metadata is temporarily unavailable, show submitted normalized reports only.
  return reports.filter(report=>dashboardProgramIsSelectedActivity(report)).map(report=>({
    ...report,

  ];

function openDashboardStudentStatus(kind, selectedDate=""){
  const defaultDate=kind==='night'?(()=>{const d=new Date(today);d.setDate(today.getDate()-1);return dateKey(d);})():dateKey(today);
  const basisDate=selectedDate||defaultDate;
  const official=officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(basisDate).map(r=>({...r,dashboardStatusType:dashboardOfficialAttendanceLabel(r,'공결')}));
  const late=(readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(r=>uepComparableDate(r.date||r.day)===basisDate).map(r=>({...r,dashboardStatusType:dashboardOfficialAttendanceLabel(r,'공지각')}));
  const gradeReportRows=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(dashboardReportStatusRows());
  const allReportGroups=dashboardReportGroups(gradeReportRows);
  const reportGroupWithRows=(group,rows)=>({...group,rows,programs:[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.programTitle||row.programName||row.title||row.activityName||'프로그램명 확인 필요').trim()).filter(Boolean))]});
  const submittedGroups=allReportGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="complete").map(group=>reportGroupWithRows(group,group.rows));
  const progressGroups=allReportGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="progress").map(group=>reportGroupWithRows(group,group.rows));
  const missingGroups=allReportGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="missing").map(group=>reportGroupWithRows(group,group.rows));
  const configs={
    late:{title:'공결·지각 학생',page:'attendance',rows:[...official,...late],attendance:true,dateSelectable:true},

    submitted:{title:'선택활동 보고서 제출완료 학생',page:'programs',rows:submittedGroups,report:true,reportGrouped:true},
    progress:{title:'선택활동 보고서 제출중 학생',page:'programs',rows:progressGroups,report:true,reportGrouped:true},
    report:{title:'선택활동 보고서 미제출 학생',page:'programs',rows:missingGroups,report:true,reportGrouped:true},

    absent:{title:'오늘 프로그램 불참 학생',page:'programs',rows:(readonlyCache?.programAttendance||[]).filter(r=>uepComparableDate(r.date||r.day)===basisDate&&/불참|결석|미참여/.test(String(r.status||r.result||'')))}
  };

function bindDashboardLayoutEditing() {
  const grid = $(".dashboard-grid.schoolboard-layout");
  if (!grid) return;
  const cards = [...grid.querySelectorAll(".widget-configurable[data-widget]")];
  if (state.settings.layoutLocked) return;
  cards.forEach((card) => {
    card.draggable = true;
    if (!card.querySelector(".widget-edit-strip")) {
      const strip = document.createElement("div");
      strip.className = "widget-edit-strip";
      strip.innerHTML = `<span>⋮⋮ 이동</span><button data-widget-width-cycle title="크기 변경">↔</button><button data-widget-hide title="숨기기">×</button>`;
      card.appendChild(strip);
      strip.querySelector("[data-widget-width-cycle]").onclick = async (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        const key = card.dataset.widget;
        const current = state.settings.widgetSettings[key]?.width || "auto";
        const next = current === "auto" ? "wide" : current === "wide" ? "fixed" : "auto";
        state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = { ...state.settings.widgetSettings[key], width: next };
        await save();
        render("dashboard");
      };
      strip.querySelector("[data-widget-hide]").onclick = async (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        const key = card.dataset.widget;
        state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = { ...state.settings.widgetSettings[key], visible: false };
        await save();
        render("dashboard");
      };
    }
    card.ondragstart = (event) => {
      event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move";
      event.dataTransfer.setData("text/widget", card.dataset.widget);
      card.classList.add("widget-dragging");
    };
    card.ondragend = () => card.classList.remove("widget-dragging");
    card.ondragover = (event) => { event.preventDefault(); event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "move"; card.classList.add("widget-drop-target"); };
    card.ondragleave = () => card.classList.remove("widget-drop-target");
    card.ondrop = async (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      card.classList.remove("widget-drop-target");
      const sourceKey = event.dataTransfer.getData("text/widget");
      const targetKey = card.dataset.widget;
      if (!sourceKey || sourceKey === targetKey) return;
      const order = [...state.settings.dashboardOrder];
      const from = order.indexOf(sourceKey), to = order.indexOf(targetKey);
      if (from < 0 || to < 0) return;
      order.splice(from, 1); order.splice(to, 0, sourceKey);
      state.settings.dashboardOrder = order;
      await save();
      render("dashboard");
      toast("위젯 순서를 저장했습니다.");
    };
  });
}

function bindDashboardLayoutEditing() {
  const grid = $(".dashboard-grid.schoolboard-layout");
  if (!grid) return;
  const cards = [...grid.querySelectorAll(".widget-configurable[data-widget]")];
  if (state.settings.layoutLocked) return;
  cards.forEach((card) => {
    card.draggable = true;
    if (!card.querySelector(".widget-edit-strip")) {
      const strip = document.createElement("div");
      strip.className = "widget-edit-strip";
      strip.innerHTML = `<span>⋮⋮ 이동</span><button data-widget-width-cycle title="크기 변경">↔</button><button data-widget-hide title="숨기기">×</button>`;
      card.appendChild(strip);
      strip.querySelector("[data-widget-width-cycle]").onclick = async (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        const key = card.dataset.widget;
        const current = state.settings.widgetSettings[key]?.width || "auto";
        const next = current === "auto" ? "wide" : current === "wide" ? "fixed" : "auto";
        state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = { ...state.settings.widgetSettings[key], width: next };
        await save();
        render("dashboard");
      };
      strip.querySelector("[data-widget-hide]").onclick = async (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        const key = card.dataset.widget;
        state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = { ...state.settings.widgetSettings[key], visible: false };
        await save();
        render("dashboard");
      };
    }
    card.ondragstart = (event) => {
      event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move";
      event.dataTransfer.setData("text/widget", card.dataset.widget);
      card.classList.add("widget-dragging");
    };
    card.ondragend = () => card.classList.remove("widget-dragging");
    card.ondragover = (event) => { event.preventDefault(); event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "move"; card.classList.add("widget-drop-target"); };
    card.ondragleave = () => card.classList.remove("widget-drop-target");
    card.ondrop = async (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      card.classList.remove("widget-drop-target");
      const sourceKey = event.dataTransfer.getData("text/widget");
      const targetKey = card.dataset.widget;
      if (!sourceKey || sourceKey === targetKey) return;
      const order = [...state.settings.dashboardOrder];
      const from = order.indexOf(sourceKey), to = order.indexOf(targetKey);
      if (from < 0 || to < 0) return;
      order.splice(from, 1); order.splice(to, 0, sourceKey);
      state.settings.dashboardOrder = order;
      await save();
      render("dashboard");
      toast("위젯 순서를 저장했습니다.");
    };
  });
}

async function sensitiveHash(value){
  const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
  const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
function sensitiveSecurityConfig(){
  try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY)||'{}')||{};}catch{return {};}
}

}

function calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event) {
  const target = String(event?.target || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const detail = String(event?.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\(?1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]?\s*2?\s*학년부[^)]*\)?/g, " ");
  if (/학부모/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
  if (/교사|교직원/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
  const text = target || detail;
  return /(?:^|[^0-9])1\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]\s*2\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /1\s*[~-]\s*3\s*학년/.test(text)
    || /전교생|전체\s*학생/.test(text);
}

function calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(event) {
  const text = `${event?.title || ""} ${event?.target || ""} ${event?.detail || ""}`;
  if (/(정기고사|기말고사|중간고사|방학식|개학식|교직원회의|부장교사회의|운영위원회|석식\s*[Xx]|급식제공|시간표\s*운영|교실개방|생활기록부.*점검|입찰|폐지\(|폐기물|제안서.*제출기간)/.test(text)) return false;
  return /(프로그램|특강|체험|교육|설명회|공연|상담|탐방|캠프|연수|활동|동아리|수련|수학여행|대회|촬영|학습코칭|석학과의 만남)/.test(text);
}

function calendarEventVisualKind(event) {
  if (/개인/.test(String(event?.type || ""))) return "personal";
  if (calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(event)) return "program";
  return "school";
}

function calendarEventMatchesFilter(event) {
  if (calendarFilter === "all") return true;
  if (calendarFilter === "pending") return calendarEventIsPending(event);
  return calendarEventVisualKind(event) === calendarFilter;
}

function calendarDisplayTitle(event) {
  const title=String(event?.title||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  const raw=String(event?.originalDetail||event?.detail||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
  const parts=raw.split(/[\n/]+/).map(x=>x.replace(/^\([^)]*\)\s*/,'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const looksGeneric = !title || /^(?:\(?\d+[-~]?\d*교시\)?|\(?\d+[-~]\d+교시\)?|~|\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?:[-~]\d{1,2}:\d{2})?|\d+학년|각 반|전체)$/.test(title);
  const candidate=parts.find(x=>!/(?:^\d{1,2}:\d{2}|^\d+학년$|교시$|희망학생|전체$|각 반|교실$|세미나실$|고당예관$|확정$|예정$|예약$|부$)/.test(x));
  if (looksGeneric && candidate) return candidate;
  return title || candidate || '일정';
}

function calendarShortTitle(title, max = 18) {
  const value = String(title || "일정").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return value.length > max ? `${value.slice(0, max)}…` : value;
}

function calendarDayTone(key) {
  const date = new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const weekday = date.getDay();
  const holiday = attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(key);
  return {
    holiday,
    saturday: weekday === 6 && !holiday,
    sunday: weekday === 0 && !holiday,
    className: holiday ? "holiday" : weekday === 6 ? "saturday" : weekday === 0 ? "sunday" : "weekday",
  };
}

function calendarSelectedDayMarkup() {
  const events = schoolEventsForDate(calendarSelectedDate).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter);
  const date = new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate}T12:00:00`);
  const label = `${date.getMonth() + 1}월 ${date.getDate()}일 ${weekdayLabelFromKey(calendarSelectedDate, true)}`;

function schoolEventsForDate(key) {
  return allSchoolEvents().filter((event) => {
    const start = String(event.date || "");
    const end = String(event.endDate || event.date || "");
    return start && key >= start && key <= end;
  });
}

function allSchoolEvents() {
  const manualRows = Array.isArray(state.settings?.manualCalendar) ? state.settings.manualCalendar : [];
  const manual = manualRows.map((row, index) => ({
    id: `manual-${index}`,
    date: row[0],
    target: row[1],
    time: row[2],
    place: row[3],
    title: row[4],
    owner: row[5],
    detail: `대상: ${row[1]} / 시간: ${row[2]} / 장소: ${row[3]} / 프로그램명: ${row[4]} / 부서·담당자: ${row[5]}`,
    source: "직접 붙여넣기",
    type: /마감|제출|입찰|종료/.test(row.join(" ")) ? "마감" : /시험|방학|수련|학력평가/.test(row.join(" ")) ? "학사" : "업무",
  }));
  const connected = Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.schoolCalendar) ? readonlyCache.schoolCalendar : [];
  const connectedKeys=new Set(connected.map(x=>`${x.date}|${x.title}|${x.time||""}`));
  return [...connected, ...manual.filter(x=>!connectedKeys.has(`${x.date}|${x.title}|${x.time||""}`))];
}

const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
function defaultNightStudyRules(){
  return [
    {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
    {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
  ];
}
function readNightStudyRules(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
  catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}
}
function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  for(const r of rules){
    if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
    if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  }
  return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
}

function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));
  const reportRows=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(dashboardReportStatusRows());
  const targetGroups=dashboardReportGroups(reportRows);
  const submitted=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="complete");
  const progress=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="progress");
  const missing=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="missing");
  const scope=dashboardGradeScope();
  const currentStudents=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(readonlyCache?.students||[]);
  const officialCount=new Set(official.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const lateCount=new Set(late.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightCount=new Set(night.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightTotal=currentStudents.length||259;
  const metrics=[
    ['late','공결/지각',`${officialCount}/${lateCount}`,'attendance',`공결 ${officialCount}명 · 지각 ${lateCount}명`,'명'],
    ['night','야자출결',`${nightCount}/${nightTotal}`,'attendance','야자 출결 학생','명'],
    ['submitted','제출완료',submitted.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 제출완료 학생','명'],
    ['progress','제출중',progress.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 일부 제출 학생','명'],
    ['report','미제출',missing.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 미제출 학생','명']
  ];
  return `<div class="uep-student-scope"><span>조회 기준 · <b>${escapeHtml(scope.label)}</b></span><strong>선택활동 대상 <b>${targetGroups.length}</b>명</strong><em>완료 ${submitted.length}명 · 제출중 ${progress.length}명 · 미제출 ${missing.length}명</em></div><div class="uep-student-kpis">${metrics.map(([kind,label,count,page,desc,unit])=>`<button class="kpi-${kind}" data-dashboard-student-status="${kind}" data-page="${page}" title="${desc} 조회"><span>${label}</span><b>${count}</b><small>${kind==='late'?desc:unit}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function dashboardStatusStudentGroups(rows,{report=false,attendance=false}={}){
  const map=new Map();
  (rows||[]).forEach((source,index)=>{
    const row=report?source:normalizeDashboardStudentRow(source);
    const key=String(row.studentRef?.id||row.studentId||row.studentNo||`${row.classLabel}|${row.name}|${index}`);
    if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{...row,key,rows:[],programs:[...(row.programs||[])],statusLabels:[]});
    const group=map.get(key);group.rows.push(...(row.rows||[row]));
    if(report){(row.programs||[]).forEach(x=>{if(!group.programs.includes(x))group.programs.push(x);});}
    if(attendance){
      const label=String(source.dashboardStatusType||source.detailType||source.type||source.statusType||'').trim();
      if(label&&!group.statusLabels.includes(label))group.statusLabels.push(label);
    }
  });
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||''),'ko'));
}
function dashboardStatusClassNo(row){
  const gc=recordGradeClass(row.studentRef||row);
  if(gc.classNo)return String(Number(gc.classNo));
  const label=String(row.classLabel||row.className||'');
  const match=label.match(/(?:^|[-\s])([1-9])(?:반)?$/);
  if(match)return String(Number(match[1]));
  const studentNo=String(row.studentNo||row.studentRef?.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(/^1[1-9]\d{2}$/.test(studentNo))return studentNo.slice(1,2);
  return '';
}

function dashboardStatusStudentGroups(rows,{report=false,attendance=false}={}){
  const map=new Map();
  (rows||[]).forEach((source,index)=>{
    const row=report?source:normalizeDashboardStudentRow(source);
    const key=String(row.studentRef?.id||row.studentId||row.studentNo||`${row.classLabel}|${row.name}|${index}`);
    if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{...row,key,rows:[],programs:[...(row.programs||[])],statusLabels:[]});
    const group=map.get(key);group.rows.push(...(row.rows||[row]));
    if(report){(row.programs||[]).forEach(x=>{if(!group.programs.includes(x))group.programs.push(x);});}
    if(attendance){
      const label=String(source.dashboardStatusType||source.detailType||source.type||source.statusType||'').trim();
      if(label&&!group.statusLabels.includes(label))group.statusLabels.push(label);
    }
  });
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||''),'ko'));
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

}

      if (!validateServiceAccount(credentials)) return { ok: false, reason: "Google 서비스 계정 JSON 형식이 아닙니다." };
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true, credentials });
      await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(), credentials);
      return {
        ok: true,
        account: credentials.client_email,
        sourceName: UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,
        preview: data.summary,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: `${error.message || "구글시트 연결 실패"} 서비스 계정 이메일에 UEP 구글시트 보기 권한이 있는지 확인하세요.` };
    }
  });
  ipcMain.handle("google:previewOperationalSync", async () => {try{return await fetchOperationalData();}catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||'UEP 운영자료 갱신 실패'};}});
  ipcMain.handle("google:previewReadonlySync", async () => {
    try {
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true });
      return { ok: true, syncedAt: data.syncedAt, preview: data.summary, nightPreview: data.summary, sourceName: data.sourceName };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트 새로고침 실패" };
    }

    try { return { ok: true, data: await fetchLiveData() }; }
    catch (error) { return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트를 읽지 못했습니다." }; }

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}

async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
  let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
  try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
}
async function schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges){
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)throw schoolReadError('UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
  const result=await schoolReadPost('batch-read',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId,spreadsheetId,ranges});
  if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BATCH_FAILED');
  return result.valueRanges||[];
}

let googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:"",expiresAt:0};

function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
  if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
  let response;
  try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
  catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
  const policy=await response.json();
  const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
  if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return clientId;
}

let googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:"",expiresAt:0};

function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
  if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
  let response;
  try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
  catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
  const policy=await response.json();
  const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
  if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return clientId;
}
function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
  const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
}
function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
  const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
  const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
  return {version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toString('base64'),iv:iv.toString('base64'),tag:cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),data:encrypted.toString('base64')};
}

}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assi

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.

  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);

    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}

      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`)

function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:bat

tion", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {

  });

  }
  return response.json();
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

nc function sheetsRequest(token,url,options={}) {
  const response=await fetch(url,{...options,headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"content-type":"application/json",...(options.headers||{})}});
  if(!response.ok){let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""}catch{};throw new Error(`Google Sheets 쓰기 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`)}
  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;

}

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const key = recoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const payload = {
    version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
    salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
  };
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
}

  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}
async function saveStudentContact(payload={}) {
  const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
  const field=String(payload.field||"");
  if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
  const role=String(payload.role||"");
  if(!["admin","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
  if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
  if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
  const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
  let fieldCol=-1;
  for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
  if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
  let dataRow=-1;
  for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
  const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
  if(dataRow<0){
    const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
    row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
    const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
  }else{
    const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
    const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
    await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
  }
  if(liveDataCache?.students){liveDataCache={...liveDataCache,students:liveDataCache.students.map(student=>String(student.id||'')===studentId?{...student,[field]:value}:student)};liveDataFetchedAt=Date.now();}
  return {ok:true,contactPatch:{studentId,field,value}};
}
